6	
7	<h3 align="center">Privacy-first encrypted cloud storage with decentralized persistence</h3>
8	
9	---
10	
11	## What is CipherBox
12	
13	CipherBox is a technology demonstrator for privacy-first cloud storage. All encryption happens
14	client-side — the server never sees plaintext data, file names, or encryption keys. Files are stored
15	on IPFS for decentralized persistence, keys are derived deterministically via Web3Auth so the same
16	user always gets the same vault regardless of login method, and data can be exported for independent
17	recovery without CipherBox.
18	
19	## Acknowledgements
20	
21	This project is inspired by discussions and planning while working on
22	[ChainSafe Files](https://github.com/chainsafe/ui-monorepo). A massive shout-out to all the
23	colleagues who worked on the original ChainSafe Files project.
24	
25	## Features
26	
27	- **Authentication** — Web3Auth MPC Core Kit: email OTP, Google OAuth, magic link, external wallet
28	  (SIWE). MFA via device factor with BIP39 recovery phrase.
29	- **Encryption** — Client-side AES-256-GCM for files and metadata. ECIES secp256k1 key wrapping
30	  (per-file, per-folder random keys). AES-256-CTR streaming for large media files. Web Worker
```

90	
91	**Rationale:** 100 MB balances usability with memory constraints. AES-256-GCM requires the full plaintext in memory for authentication tag computation. Large media files use AES-256-CTR which supports streaming, but the 100 MB limit applies uniformly. The Web Worker encryption offload helps with CPU blocking but does not change the memory requirement.
92	
93	### Storage Quota
94	
95	| Limit       | Value   | Enforcement                                        | Constant                            |
96	| ----------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- |
97	| Total quota | 500 MiB | Server-side (`vault.service.ts`), client pre-check | `QUOTA_LIMIT_BYTES` / `QUOTA_BYTES` |
98	
99	**Rationale:** Technology demonstrator limit. Quota tracks encrypted blob sizes (not plaintext sizes). BYO-IPFS users bypass this limit (advisory only) since they manage their own storage.
100	
101	## Folder Structure
102	
103	### Depth Limit
104

95	**Storage:** IPFS (as JSON envelope `{iv, data}`), addressed via the folder's IPNS name.
96	
97	**Source files:**
98	
99	- TS types: `packages/core/src/folder/types.ts:15-20`
100	- TS validator: `packages/core/src/folder/metadata.ts:38-96`
101	- Rust: `crates/core/src/folder.rs:78-84`
102	
103	**Version history:**
104	
105	| Version | Phase   | Change                                                                                                   |
106	| ------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
107	| v1      | Initial | Children contained inline `FileEntry` with `cid`, `fileKeyEncrypted`, `fileIv`, `size`, `encryptionMode` |
108	| v2      | 12.6    | Children use `FilePointer` (slim IPNS reference). Per-file metadata moved to dedicated IPNS records      |
109	
110	v1 was completely removed in Phase 11.2. The validator rejects v1 data with a `CryptoError`. This was a clean-break migration (pre-production vault wipe).
111	
112	---
113	
114	## 5. FolderChild (Union)
115	
116	Discriminated union type for children within a folder.
117	
118	| Discriminant     | Type          | Description                              |
119	| ---------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
120	| `type: 'folder'` | `FolderEntry` | Subfolder with ECIES-wrapped keys        |
121	| `type: 'file'`   | `FilePointer` | Slim reference to a per-file IPNS record |
122	
123	**Source files:**
124	
125	- TS: `packages/core/src/folder/types.ts:25`
126	- Rust: `crates/core/src/folder.rs:66-73` (serde internally tagged enum with `#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "lowercase")]`)
127	
128	---
129	
130	## 6. FolderEntry
131	
132	A subfolder child within folder metadata. Contains ECIES-wrapped keys for accessing the subfolder's own metadata.
133	
134	| Field                     | Type   | Encoding  | Required | Description                                                         |
135	| ------------------------- | ------ | --------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
136	| `type`                    | string | --        | Yes      | Always `"folder"`                                                   |
137	| `id`                      | string | UUID      | Yes      | Unique identifier for this subfolder                                |
138	| `name`                    | string | --        | Yes      | Folder name (plaintext; entire metadata blob is encrypted)          |
139	| `ipnsName`                | string | base32/36 | Yes      | IPNS name for resolving this subfolder's metadata                   |
140	| `ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted` | string | hex       | Yes      | ECIES-wrapped 64-byte Ed25519 IPNS key (161 bytes, 322 hex chars)   |
141	| `folderKeyEncrypted`      | string | hex       | Yes      | ECIES-wrapped 32-byte AES-256 folder key (129 bytes, 258 hex chars) |
142	| `createdAt`               | number | --        | Yes      | Unix timestamp in milliseconds                                      |
143	| `modifiedAt`              | number | --        | Yes      | Unix timestamp in milliseconds                                      |
144	
145	**Not independently encrypted** -- lives inside the parent `FolderMetadata` blob.
146	
147	**ECIES wrapping:** Both `folderKeyEncrypted` and `ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted` are encrypted to the vault owner's secp256k1 `publicKey`. See [VAULT_EXPORT_FORMAT.md](VAULT_EXPORT_FORMAT.md) Section 4 for the ECIES ciphertext binary format.
148	
149	**Subfolder IPNS keys:** Randomly generated Ed25519 keypairs (not HKDF-derived). This is different from root vault and per-file IPNS keys which are deterministically derived.
150	
151	**Source files:**
152	
153	- TS: `packages/core/src/folder/types.ts:31-47`
154	- Rust: `crates/core/src/folder.rs:28-45`
155	
156	**Version history:** Unchanged since initial design.
157	
158	---
159

```
210	**Storage:** IPFS (as JSON envelope `{iv, data}`), addressed via the file's own IPNS name. For new files, the IPNS keypair is randomly generated and stored in the parent's `FilePointer.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted`. For legacy files, it is derived via HKDF from `privateKey + fileId` (see [Section 15](#15-ipns-key-derivation-summary)).
211	
212	**Source files:**
213	
214	- TS types: `packages/core/src/file/types.ts:30-51`
215	- TS validator: `packages/core/src/file/metadata.ts:98-193`
216	- Rust: `crates/core/src/file.rs:166-192`
217	
218	**Version history:**
219	
220	| Version               | Phase     | Change                                                                 | Version Bumped? |
221	| --------------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- |
222	| v1 (initial)          | 12.6      | Initial per-file IPNS schema                                           | --              |
223	| v1 + `encryptionMode` | 12.6/12.1 | Optional field added, defaults to `'GCM'`. Supports AES-CTR streaming. | No              |
224	| v1 + `versions`       | 13        | Optional `VersionEntry[]` array. Omitted when empty.                   | No              |
225	
226	Both additions were additive optional fields with sensible defaults -- the version field was not bumped. This informal pattern is formalized in [METADATA_EVOLUTION_PROTOCOL.md](METADATA_EVOLUTION_PROTOCOL.md).
227	
228	**Rust serde annotations for optional fields:**
229	
230	```rust
231	#[serde(default = "default_encryption_mode")]  // defaults to "GCM"
232	pub encryption_mode: String,
233	
234	#[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
235	#[serde(default)]
236	pub versions: Option<Vec<VersionEntry>>,
237	```
238	
239	---
240	
241	## 9. VersionEntry
242	
243	A single past version of a file. Embedded in the `versions` array of `FileMetadata`. Each entry contains the full crypto context needed to independently decrypt that version's content.
244	
245	| Field              | Type               | Encoding | Required | Description                                                        |
246	| ------------------ | ------------------ | -------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
247	| `cid`              | string             | CIDv1    | Yes      | IPFS content identifier of the encrypted file for this version     |
248	| `fileKeyEncrypted` | string             | hex      | Yes      | ECIES-wrapped 32-byte AES-256 key for this version (258 hex chars) |
249	| `fileIv`           | string             | hex      | Yes      | 12-byte IV used for this version's encryption (24 hex chars)       |
250	| `size`             | number             | --       | Yes      | Original unencrypted file size in bytes                            |
251	| `timestamp`        | number             | --       | Yes      | When this version was created (Unix ms)                            |
252	| `encryptionMode`   | `'GCM'` \| `'CTR'` | --       | Yes      | Encryption mode used for this version                              |
253	
254	**Not independently encrypted** -- embedded in the parent `FileMetadata` blob.
255	
256	**Key difference from FileMetadata:** `encryptionMode` is **required** (not optional) because past versions always record the explicit encryption mode used at the time of creation.
257	
258	**Constraints:**
259	
260	- Maximum 10 versions per file
261	- 15-minute cooldown between version creation
262	
263	**Source files:**
264	
265	- TS types: `packages/core/src/file/types.ts:10-23`
266	- TS validator: `packages/core/src/file/metadata.ts:37-92`
267	- Rust: `crates/core/src/file.rs:145-160`
268	
269	**Version history:** Added in Phase 13 (File Versioning).
```

415	## 14. Cross-Implementation Parity
416	
417	TypeScript and Rust implementations must produce identical JSON for the same logical data.
418	
419	| Schema            | TypeScript          | Rust                                   | Notes                          |
420	| ----------------- | ------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
421	| FolderMetadata    | `folder/types.ts`   | `crates/core/src/folder.rs`                     | Both platforms                 |
422	| FolderChild       | `folder/types.ts`   | `crates/core/src/folder.rs` (serde tagged enum) | Both platforms                 |
423	| FolderEntry       | `folder/types.ts`   | `crates/core/src/folder.rs`                     | Both platforms                 |
424	| FilePointer       | `file/types.ts`     | `crates/core/src/folder.rs`                     | Both platforms                 |
425	| FileMetadata      | `file/types.ts`     | `crates/core/src/file.rs`                       | Both platforms                 |
426	| VersionEntry      | `file/types.ts`     | `crates/core/src/file.rs`                       | Both platforms                 |
427	| VaultKeyBlob (v2) | `vault/blob.ts`     | `crates/core/src/vault_blob.rs`                 | Both platforms (binary format) |
428	| DeviceRegistry    | `registry/types.ts` | --                                     | TypeScript only                |

Done. Everything is committed as `e01f9ea3d docs: generate and verify project documentation` on the local branch `docs/gsd-docs-update` — 28 files, +2666/−467. Not pushed; say the word and I'll push + open a PR.

## Summary

**Project type:** monorepo. 22 docs written/updated, 17 docs fact-checked against the codebase — 383+ claims verified in round 1, every failure fixed and re-verified.

**Canonical + gap docs:**

| File | Mode | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| README.md | regenerate | 18/18 (demonstrator wording removed per your ask) |
| docs/ARCHITECTURE.md | regenerate | 22/22 after 1 fix |
| docs/CONFIGURATION.md | create | 19/19 |
| docs/GETTING-STARTED.md | create | 14/14 |
| docs/DEVELOPMENT.md | supplement (+27 lines, 0 deletions) | 16/16 after 3 fixes |
| docs/TESTING.md | create | 47/47 |
| docs/DEPLOYMENT.md | create | 36/36 |
| CONTRIBUTING.md | create | 14/14 |
| SECURITY.md | create (gap) | 8/8 |
| docs/SHARING.md | create (gap) | 38/38 |

**Per-package READMEs (12):** created for the 4 apps + `tests/load`, `tests/sdk-e2e`; updated the 5 package READMEs and `tests/web-e2e` (that one was badly stale — wrong CI workflow name, removed auth pattern, phantom scripts).

**Existing-doc accuracy review:** AUTHENTICATION_ARCHITECTURE passed clean (20/20, untouched). The other six had real drift, now fixed — highlights: TEE republish interval said 3h vs actual 6h (CAPACITY, 3 places), METADATA_SCHEMAS cited `packages/crypto/` for types that live in `packages/core/` (15+ references) plus a secp256k1/Ed25519 mix-up, VAULT_EXPORT_FORMAT documented base64 keys and v1 recovery the reference tool rejects, FILESYSTEM_SPECIFICATION referenced a nonexistent `folder.service.ts`, and DATABASE_EVOLUTION_PROTOCOL was missing 2 of 16 migrations.

**Fix loop:** 2 iterations, 0 regressions, 0 remaining failures. The mid-run process restart killed the iteration-2 agents, but their edits had already landed; I applied the final 6 METADATA_SCHEMAS Rust line-range corrections by hand using verifier-confirmed locations.

**VERIFY markers (20)** flag claims not provable from the repo — 12 in CONFIGURATION, 6 in DEPLOYMENT, 2 in SECURITY (e.g. whether GitHub private vulnerability reporting is enabled, Grafana Cloud URLs, staging host provisioning). Worth a skim.

One minor reviewer note: `docker-compose.yml` defines a `someguy` delegated-routing service (port 8190) that DEVELOPMENT.md's infra table doesn't list — an omission rather than an error, left as-is.

Re-run `/gsd:docs-update --verify-only` anytime to re-audit docs against the code.

  tee-worker:
  someguy:
    image: ghcr.io/ipfs/someguy:v0.11.1
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      SOMEGUY_LISTEN_ADDRESS: 0.0.0.0:8190
      SOMEGUY_DHT: accelerated
      SOMEGUY_LIBP2P_CONNMGR_LOW: 100
      SOMEGUY_LIBP2P_CONNMGR_HIGH: 3000
      SOMEGUY_LIBP2P_MAX_MEMORY: 1073741824
      GOLOG_LOG_LEVEL: info
      GOLOG_LOG_FMT: json
    ports:
      # libp2p swarm - required for DHT participation
      - '4004:4004/tcp'
      - '4004:4004/udp'
    logging: *default-logging
    healthcheck:
      test: ['CMD-SHELL', 'curl -sf http://localhost:8190/version || exit 1']
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 10
      start_period: 60s
    deploy:
      resources:
        limits:
          memory: 2G
          cpus: '1.0'

  caddy:
    image: caddy:2-alpine
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - '80:80'
      - '443:443'
    volumes:
      - ./Caddyfile:/etc/caddy/Caddyfile
      - ./certs:/etc/caddy/certs
      - /opt/cipherbox/web:/srv/web:ro
      - caddy_data:/data
      - caddy_config:/config
    logging: *default-logging
```

  "new_string": "This starts the `cipherbox-infrastructure` compose project (containers are named `cipherbox-<service>`):\n\n| Service             | Image                          | Host Port(s)                                      | Purpose                                  |\n| :------------------ | :----------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------ | :--------------------------------------- |\n| `postgres`          | `postgres:16-alpine`           | 5432 (`DB_PORT`)                                  | Database                                 |\n| `ipfs`              | `ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0`            | 5001 (API), 8080 (gateway), 4001 tcp/udp (swarm)  | Decentralized storage (Kubo)             |\n| `redis`             | `redis:7-alpine`               | 6380 (`REDIS_PORT`) → container 6379              | BullMQ job queue                         |\n| `someguy`           | `ghcr.io/ipfs/someguy:v0.11.1` | 8190 (routing API), 4004 tcp/udp (libp2p swarm)   | Delegated IPFS routing (accelerated DHT) |\n| `mock-ipns-routing` | built from `tools/mock-ipns-routing/` | 3001 (loopback only)                        | Local IPNS resolution for dev/E2E        |\n\nNotes:\n\n- The IPFS node runs with the `server,pebbleds` datastore profile. If you have an `ipfs_data` volume created before the pebbleds switch, recreate it first: `docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml down -v --remove-orphans`.\n- The `ipfs` and `someguy` containers are each capped at 2 GB memory / 1 CPU.\n- The staging stack runs a different set of containers (adds `api`, `tee-worker`, `caddy`, `alloy`; drops `mock-ipns-routing`) — see [DEPLOYMENT.md](DEPLOYMENT.md)."

## Warnings

- **[W004]** `config.json`: invalid `model_profile` `"adaptive"` — valid values are `quality`, `balanced`, `budget`, `inherit`. (This also explains the "unknown config key" warnings gsd-tools printed all session; doc generation still resolved models fine.)
  Fix: set a valid value via `/gsd:config` or edit `.planning/config.json`.
- **[W009]** Phase `34-e2e-test-expansion-staging-baselines` has a Validation Architecture in RESEARCH.md but no VALIDATION.md.
  Fix: re-run `/gsd:plan-phase` with `--research` to regenerate.
- **[W019] ×5** Non-canonical files at `.planning/` root: `DEFERRED.md`, `ENVIRONMENTS.md`, `FEATURES.md`, `REFACTORING.md`, `SCOPING_RATIONALE.md`.
  Fix: move to `.planning/milestones/` archive subdir, or delete if stale.

None of these are auto-repairable, so `--repair` would be a no-op here. The W019 files look like deliberate project notes rather than corruption — if you want them kept, they just live in the wrong spot for the validator; say the word and I'll move them into `.planning/milestones/` (or wherever you prefer) and fix the `model_profile` value in the same pass.

/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/.claude/gsd-core/templates/README.md
# GSD Canonical Artifact Registry

This directory contains the template files for every artifact that GSD workflows officially produce. The table below is the authoritative index: **if a `.planning/` root file is not listed here, `gsd-health` will flag it as W019** (unrecognized artifact).

Agents should query this file before treating a `.planning/` file as authoritative. If the file name does not appear below, it is not a canonical GSD artifact.

---

## `.planning/` Root Artifacts

These files live directly at `.planning/` — not inside phase subdirectories.

| File               | Template          | Produced by                                    | Purpose                                                        |
| ------------------ | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROJECT.md`       | `project.md`      | `/gsd-new-project`                             | Project identity, goals, requirements summary                  |
| `ROADMAP.md`       | `roadmap.md`      | `/gsd-new-milestone`, `/gsd-new-project`       | Phase plan with milestones and progress tracking               |
| `STATE.md`         | `state.md`        | `/gsd-new-project`, `/gsd-health --repair`     | Current session state, active phase, last activity             |
| `REQUIREMENTS.md`  | `requirements.md` | `/gsd-new-milestone`                           | Functional requirements with traceability                      |
| `MILESTONES.md`    | `milestone.md`    | `/gsd-complete-milestone`                      | Log of completed milestones with accomplishments               |
| `BACKLOG.md`       | _(inline)_        | `/gsd-add-backlog`                             | Pending ideas and deferred work                                |
| `LEARNINGS.md`     | _(inline)_        | `/gsd-extract-learnings`, `/gsd-execute-phase` | Phase retrospective learnings for future plans                 |
| `THREADS.md`       | _(inline)_        | `/gsd-thread`                                  | Persistent discussion threads                                  |
| `config.json`      | `config.json`     | `/gsd-new-project`, `/gsd-health --repair`     | Project-specific GSD configuration                             |
| `CLAUDE.md`        | `claude-md.md`    | `/gsd-profile`                                 | Auto-assembled Claude Code context file                        |
| `RETROSPECTIVE.md` | _(inline)_        | `/gsd-complete-milestone`                      | Living milestone retrospective updated at each milestone close |

### Version-stamped artifacts (pattern: `vX.Y-*.md`)

| Pattern                   | Produced by            | Purpose                                 |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------- | --------------------------------------- |
| `vX.Y-MILESTONE-AUDIT.md` | `/gsd-audit-milestone` | Milestone audit report before archiving |

These files are archived to `.planning/milestones/` by `/gsd-complete-milestone`. Finding them at the `.planning/` root after completion indicates the archive step was skipped.

---
--
## Adding a New Canonical Artifact

When a new workflow produces a `.planning/` root file:

1. Add the file name to `CANONICAL_EXACT` in `gsd-core/bin/lib/artifacts.cjs`
2. Add a row to the **`.planning/` Root Artifacts** table above
3. Add the template to `gsd-core/templates/` if one exists

364	## Validation Architecture
365	
366	### Test Framework
367	
368	| Property           | Value                                                                               |
369	| ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
370	| Framework          | @playwright/test 1.57.0                                                             |
371	| Config file        | `tests/web-e2e/playwright.config.ts` (local), `playwright.load.config.ts` (staging) |
372	| Quick run command  | `cd tests/web-e2e && pnpm exec playwright test tests/<spec>.spec.ts`                |
373	| Full suite command | `cd tests/web-e2e && pnpm exec playwright test`                                     |
374	
375	### Phase Requirements -> Test Map
376	
377	No formal requirement IDs for this phase (test coverage and baseline capture). The success criteria map directly to test files:
378	
379	| Success Criterion              | Test Type                  | Verification                                 |
380	| ------------------------------ | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
381	| AES-CTR streaming playback E2E | E2E (Playwright)           | `streaming-playback.spec.ts` runs green      |
382	| Batch download E2E             | E2E (Playwright)           | `batch-download.spec.ts` runs green          |
383	| Media preview E2E              | E2E (Playwright)           | `media-preview.spec.ts` runs green           |
384	| Shared deleteAccount teardown  | Code inspection + test run | All specs' afterAll hooks call deleteAccount |
385	| BYO-IPFS load baselines        | Manual staging run         | Baseline numbers documented                  |
386	| Staging metrics baselines      | Manual staging run         | Journey timing + load test results captured  |
387	
388	### Sampling Rate
389	
390	- **Per task commit:** `cd tests/web-e2e && pnpm exec playwright test tests/<new-spec>.spec.ts`
391	- **Per wave merge:** `cd tests/web-e2e && pnpm exec playwright test` (full suite)
392	- **Phase gate:** Full suite green + staging runs documented

405	## Environment Availability
406	
407	| Dependency             | Required By                   | Available      | Version       | Fallback                                     |
408	| ---------------------- | ----------------------------- | -------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
409	| Playwright             | All E2E tests                 | Y              | 1.57.0        | --                                           |
410	| Node.js                | Test runner                   | Y              | (host)        | --                                           |
411	| ffmpeg                 | Media fixture generation      | Check needed   | --            | Use binary stubs with media extensions       |
412	| Staging VPS            | Load/baseline tests           | Y              | 76.13.151.200 | --                                           |
413	| Grafana Faro endpoint  | Metrics baseline verification | Y (on staging) | --            | Document metrics without visual verification |
414	| External IPFS provider | BYO load baselines            | Uncertain      | --            | Document test plan; defer actual run         |
415	
416	**Missing dependencies with no fallback:**
417	
418	- None that block implementation. All new test specs run against local dev environment.
419	
420	**Missing dependencies with fallback:**
421	
422	- ffmpeg for media fixtures -- fallback is creating binary files with media extensions (tests verify dialog opens but skip codec-dependent assertions)
423	- External IPFS provider for BYO load test -- fallback is documenting the test plan and running when infrastructure available
424	
425	## Open Questions
426	
427	1. **BYO-IPFS Load Test Execution**
428	   - What we know: No external IPFS provider infrastructure is set up for staging. Phase 21 deferred BYO benchmarks for this reason.
429	   - What's unclear: Whether an external IPFS provider (Pinata, Filebase, etc.) is available or budgeted for testing.
430	   - Recommendation: Create the test harness/scripts ready to run, document what provider setup is needed, flag as manual execution when provider is available. Do not block phase completion on this.
431	
432	2. **Media Fixtures: ffmpeg vs Binary Stubs**
433	   - What we know: Real media files work best for codec/playback assertions. Binary stubs with media extensions work for dialog/element rendering assertions.
434	   - What's unclear: Whether ffmpeg is installed on the dev machine.
435	   - Recommendation: Check `command -v ffmpeg` at plan execution time. If available, generate real fixtures. If not, use binary stubs and limit assertions to modal rendering (not playback events).
436	
437	3. **Staging Access for Baseline Capture**
438	   - What we know: Staging is at `app-staging.cipherbox.cc` / `api-staging.cipherbox.cc`. SSH access to VPS at 76.13.151.200.
439	   - What's unclear: Whether latest code (through Phase 33) is deployed to staging.
440	   - Recommendation: Verify staging deployment status before running baselines. If not up to date, deploy first.
441	
442	## Sources
443	
444	### Primary (HIGH confidence)
445	
446	- Direct codebase inspection of all 12 existing E2E spec files in `tests/web-e2e/tests/`
447	- Direct inspection of page objects, utils, and fixture directories
448	- Direct inspection of production components: `VideoPlayerDialog.tsx`, `AudioPlayerDialog.tsx`, `PdfPreviewDialog.tsx`, `decrypt-sw.ts`, `streaming-crypto.service.ts`, `useStreamingPreview.ts`
449	- `playwright.config.ts` and `playwright.load.config.ts` configuration analysis
450	- `load-test.spec.ts` `deleteAccount()` pattern (lines 169-204)
451	- `useFileBrowserActions.ts` `handleBatchDownload` implementation (lines 430-446)
452	
453	### Secondary (MEDIUM confidence)
454	
455	- Playwright 1.57.0 documentation for Service Worker testing capabilities (from training data, version verified via installed package)
456	
457	## Metadata
458	
459	**Confidence breakdown:**
460	
461	- Standard stack: HIGH - all libraries already installed, patterns established across 12 specs
462	- Architecture: HIGH - patterns directly observed in existing codebase, no new patterns needed
463	- Pitfalls: HIGH - pitfalls identified from actual code inspection (CTR threshold logic, batch download non-zip behavior, SW registration lifecycle)
464	
465	**Research date:** 2026-03-29
466	**Valid until:** 2026-04-28 (stable -- testing infrastructure, not fast-moving dependencies)

{
  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/.claude/projects/-Users-myankelev-Code-random-cipher-box/memory/project-gsd-commit-helper-false-negative.md",
  "content": "---\nname: project-gsd-commit-helper-false-negative\ndescription: gsd-tools \"query commit\" reports committed:false/commit_failed even when the commit lands — verify with git log, don't retry\nmetadata:\n  type: project\n---\n\n`node .claude/gsd-core/bin/gsd-tools.cjs query commit \"msg\" --files ...` frequently returns `{\"committed\": false, \"reason\": \"commit_failed\"}` with lint-staged's TTY spinner output captured in `error` — while the commit actually succeeds (observed twice on 2026-06-12, commits e01f9ea3d-era and 965e60894).\n\n**Why:** the helper appears to misparse lint-staged's ANSI progress output on stderr as a failure signal.\n\n**How to apply:** after running the helper, verify with `git log -1 --format='%h %s'` instead of trusting the JSON. Never blindly retry the commit on `commit_failed` — a retry would create a duplicate/empty commit. Related: [[project-1password-ssh-signing-hangs]] (run the helper unsandboxed in background Bash for signing).\n"
}

1	# CipherBox Project Memory
2	
3	## Markdown Lint Rules (pre-commit hook)
4	
5	The repo enforces `markdownlint` via lint-staged on commit. Common violations:
6	
7	- **MD036**: Don't use `**bold text**` as a heading — use proper `###` headings instead
8	- **MD031/MD032**: Blank lines required around fenced code blocks and lists
9	- Italic footers like `*Last updated: ...*` trigger MD036 — use plain text instead
10	
11	When spawning sub-agents that write `.md` files and commit, include this warning:
12	> Markdownlint enforced on commit. Use headings not bold-as-heading. Blank lines around code blocks and lists.
13	
14	## Commit Hooks
15	
16	- **lint-staged**: Runs markdownlint, prettier on `.md` files; eslint on `.ts/.tsx`
17	- **commitlint**: Conventional commits (`feat:`, `fix:`, etc.) + custom rule rejecting parens in subject. As of 2026-06-10 the `.husky/commit-msg` hook is an Entire CLI wrapper that does NOT run commitlint locally — enforcement is via PR-title CI (`pr-title.yml`). Still follow the format.
18	- Package: `@commitlint/cli` + `@commitlint/config-conventional` (had to install manually 2026-02-11)
19	
20	## GSD Sub-Agent Tips
21	
22	- [GSD subagents must not run full test suites](feedback-gsd-subagents-no-test-runs.md) — checker agents with Bash will run concurrent vitest suites and starve RAM; constrain prompts to "static analysis only"
23	- Sub-agents do NOT automatically receive `~/.claude/learnings/LEARNINGS.md` or project memory
24	- Include critical constraints (markdownlint rules, commit format) in agent prompts explicitly
25	- Research agents write to `.planning/research/` which is gitignored — commits need explicit `git add`
26	
27	## Git Commit Signing
28	
29	- [1Password SSH signing hangs](project-1password-ssh-signing-hangs.md) — git commit needs unsandboxed background Bash; hung signers wedge 1Password, restart app to recover; PRs enforce signatures so never use --no-gpg-sign
30	
31	## Infra Context
32	
33	- [Phala credits expected](project-phala-credits-expected.md) — staging TEE may return to Phala Cloud; don't flag the inert PHALA_* GitHub staging env entries as orphaned in audits
34	
35	## gh CLI Auth
36	
37	- [gh requires env -u GITHUB_TOKEN](feedback_gh_auth.md) — always prefix gh commands with `env -u GITHUB_TOKEN`
38	
```
